1. `auth.saml_providers` CHECK named `metadata_url not empty` is `CHECK (metadata_url = NULL::text OR char_length(metadata_url) > 0)`. SQL comparison using `= NULL` produces UNKNOWN for an empty string; CHECK accepts UNKNOWN, so the check **does not reject empty `metadata_url`**. Intended null-or-nonempty predicate would require `IS NULL`, but no production amendment authorized.
2. `auth.sso_providers` CHECK named `resource_id not empty` repeats the same error: `CHECK (resource_id = NULL::text OR char_length(resource_id) > 0)`. Empty `resource_id` is accepted by this constraint. Read-only SELECT directly confirmed NULL expression and `IS NOT FALSE = true`; full catalog constraint text retrieved. Only these two existing provider CHECKs match the exact `= NULL` pattern across the inspected Auth/Realtime/Storage/Vault namespaces.

**Scope/impact:** These are confirmed ineffective database CHECK predicates, NOT proven malformed records, API/security exploits or absent GoTrue service-layer validation. Both objects belong to Supabase Auth/GoTrue; don't create unauthorized app migration altering vendor-owned Auth schema, and do not touch production. ## November 8, 2026 UTC — Run96d: provider-managed Vault crypto volatility review [skip netlify]

While cross-verifying the completed 248 nonpg original declarations against official documentation and upstream Supabase Vault source, identified **verified catalog-level inconsistency but unverified runtime impact**: `vault._crypto_aead_det_noncegen()` is declared `IMMUTABLE` (live `pg_proc.provolatile='i'`), while `vault.secrets.nonce` invokes the generator as its default. A generator of fresh nonces should not be immutable. PostgreSQL 17 official volatility docs allow constant-folding/no-argument immutable calls, creating a theoretical nonce-reuse risk if this path is used for multiple secrets under the same key. Upstream `supabase/vault` `supabase_vault.sql` source SHA `de0b3bee5abb84a032877e85c52f0041ca7f636e` marks the C function immutable too; compiled binary not exposed. See immutable source-evidence receipt `docs/audits/issue509-provider-vault-nonce-volatility-review-20261108-run96d.json` for exact declarations, links, unsupported assumptions and next steps.

**Live impact check limited to safe aggregate only:** `vault.secrets` row count **0**, distinct nonce count **0**; no existing stored Vault secrets affected in this project, no duplicate nonce claim. Vault schema USAGE and create/update EXECUTE are denied to both anon/authenticated. No Vault secret contents/keys/nonces fetched, no nonce generator called, no production DDL/DML. Record as **provider-owned security review candidate** for private Supabase vendor confirmation **before Vault adoption**; no unilateral production code alteration or unsupported app migration. Previous full function raw review remains 248/248, overall baseline IN_PROGRESS, native C/runtime proof UNVERIFIED. This finding supersedes no existing owner notification policy; report only as a newly changed security-risk candidate, not as a demonstrated leak.
